Any Lord who discovers the site must decide whether they accept it too.\n\nSpecific assistance that advances the work: Quietus samples from Hollow-edge incursions; Lunarian fragments not yet sourced by Lantern Quarter mortals; protection against Lord agents who will eventually find and try to destroy the work; political cover from a Lord willing to openly patron the site at the cost of their own standing.\n\nIf the Quietus presence is solved through another path before the weapon is completed, Pomor can stop. She had been studying the Hollow's substrate anomalies for months from a research camp at the Fade Approach -- the Hollow's millennia-old low-mana behaviours are well-known to researchers of her tradition. Days ago, when Black's Passage opened and Quietus organisms began emerging in numbers her web-instruments could not track, she abandoned the camp. Most of her equipment was lost. She salvaged what she could and rebuilt her work in a smaller form at the Refuge Camp.\n\nTsuchigumo is willing to talk to anyone who asks. The substrate disturbance made it cleaner to track, but the signal itself was there well before the wound\n- She does NOT know what Quietus is. She is studying the wound, not its inhabitants.\n- She believes the signal in the south may be connected to whatever made the scar in the first place, and that reaching it might matter more than sealing the wound by force\n\nIf players show genuine interest, Tsuchigumo will offer one of two paths depending on how the conversation runs:\n1.
